# llm-d: Agent Context

[Overview](index.md) · [Agent Context](agent-context.md) · [Extracted Data](details.md) · [Network](network.md) · [RBAC](rbac.md) · [Security](security.md) · [Dataflow](dataflow.md)

Static-analysis context for repository-level code navigation. Use the source links to verify details. Call edges are analyzer-resolved relationships, not complete runtime traces.

## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- llm-d is a Kubernetes component.
- deploying 6 workload(s) (llm-d-coordinator, fma-requester, render, render, interactive-pod, mooncake-master-store) with 6 container(s).
- exposing 8 service(s) on ports\: 50051, 50052, 6379, 8000, 8080, 9003.
- Dockerfile EXPOSE ports\: 80, 8081.
- with 1 HorizontalPodAutoscaler(s).
- connecting to external services\: requests, urllib.
- runtime dependencies\: PostgreSQL, Redis, atenet-router.
- with 12 ingress route(s).
- Python k8s client with 4 API call(s).
- integrates with Istio, Kueue, Prometheus via labels/annotations.
- references components\: vllm (import).
- mounts 2 ConfigMap(s) as volumes\: llm-d-coordinator-config, mooncake-master-store-config.
